# 概率模型、过程与尾界

可按题名、原件哈希或技能名检索。

## A general stochastic maximum principle for mixed relaxed-singular control problems

- 原件 221c88024e7851943550b03a0fecb03b18b3ed0efecc0618d67166d8b2f708ae。arXiv:0801.4669v4 (28 Aug 2008; PDF dated 18 Aug 2021)。物理页 1, 2。
- processes。Abstract and introduction, pp. 1–2。
- 引用核对。The manuscript announces Pontryagin-type necessary conditions for optimal controls with a measure-valued relaxed component and a singular component in nonlinear SDEs, without requiring a convex control domain.。
- 适用范围。It gives necessary conditions for the stated mixed relaxed-singular model, not a generic dynamic-programming or HJB comparison result.。

## A multi-dimensional version of Lamperti’s relation and the Matsumoto-Yor processes

- 原件 cf022f495a45ff819d5bea98886b370d9b80dbb5b998b7cdeb2724daff6da2ea。arXiv:2306.02158v1 (3 Jun 2023)。物理页 1, 2。
- processes。pp. 1–2, abstract and introduction。
- 邻近材料。Extends Lamperti/Matsumoto–Yor relations to interacting Brownian semimartingales, yielding an opposite-drift theorem and an intertwining with interacting geometric Brownian motions.。
- 适用范围。It concerns interacting Brownian/Lamperti structures, not inhomogeneous jump-generator calculations.。

## A New Application of Hoeffding’s Inequality Can Give Traders Early Warning of Financial Regime Change

- 原件 2c071834af4b4655689e171845ec395a1bbc21e8fb724bfcf01ffcf123dfa3cb。arXiv:2512.08851v1 (9 Dec 2025)。物理页 1, 2。
- processes。PDF p. 1, introduction immediately after displayed inequality (1.1)。
- 待核。The preprint proposes a financial-regime-warning application based on Hoeffding-style deviations for bounded observations.。
- 适用范围。The inspected text says the standard Hoeffding inequality applies regardless of serial correlation, which is not a standard Hoeffding hypothesis. Do not use it as H05 evidence without a precise dependent-data theorem and proof check.。

- isoperimetry of regular planar graphs。Abstract and §1 definitions (PDF pp. 1–2)。
- 已有技能。Determines two isoperimetric constants explicitly for infinite plane-embedded graphs with common vertex degree d and face degree f.。
- 适用范围。Only applies to the (d,f)-regular planar embedded setting; it does not give an isoperimetric result for general random planar graphs.。
- 相关技能。[probability-planar-isoperimetry](../../../../probability-planar-isoperimetry/SKILL.md)。

- 原件 2413a92d920b972a562cae22235c1554d4d0cb09e8bfa1066a4876efc26b58f1。Discrete & Computational Geometry 28 (2002), pp.313–330, DOI 10.1007/s00454-002-0694-2。物理页 1, 2。
- isoperimetry of plane graphs。p.313 abstract and introduction。
- 已有技能。For infinite locally finite one-ended plane graphs with minimum degree δ≥7, the authors define an area/boundary isoperimetric constant and prove stated lower and upper bounds for its extremal value h(δ).。
- 适用范围。The estimates are for the paper’s one-ended plane-graph and minimum-degree framework; they are not Cheeger bounds for arbitrary planar graphs or random maps.。

- Extended Skorokhod maps for reflected diffusions。Abstract on p. 1; Definition 1.2 of the extended Skorokhod problem on p. 5.。
- 已有技能。Given a closed domain, boundary reflection-direction cones, and a cadlag input, the paper defines an extended Skorokhod problem/map that allows reflected paths whose constraining term need not have bounded variation, and establishes results under its closed-graph conditions.。
- 适用范围。Domain geometry, the set-valued direction field, and the ESP versus classical SP distinction are indispensable. This is a pathwise construction result, not a generic coupling/coalescence theorem.。
- 相关技能。[probability-reflected-diffusion-coupling](../../../../probability-reflected-diffusion-coupling/SKILL.md)。

## Simultaneous Boundary Hitting by Coupled Reflected Brownian Motions

- 原件 4ac48264a39f5ab59c8b904dd0bc5daf8bfd688bf3e795c7139d929163282c17。arXiv:1812.08355v1 (20 Dec 2018)。物理页 1, 2。
- reflected Brownian coupling。p.1 abstract and Section 1; p.2 equation (2.1)。
- 已有技能。For synchronized or mirror-coupled reflected Brownian motions, the paper proves specified simultaneous-boundary and local-time-singularity phenomena and gives a wedge counterexample to an overly simple mirror construction.。
- 适用范围。Claims assume the stated reflected-Brownian couplings and regular domains; this does not establish generic coupling or mixing bounds for arbitrary diffusions.。
- 相关技能。[probability-reflected-diffusion-coupling](../../../../probability-reflected-diffusion-coupling/SKILL.md)。

- obliquely reflected Skorokhod problems。Abstract, Definition 1.1, and §1 (PDF pp. 1–2)。
- 已有技能。For orthant reflection matrix R, addresses critical spectral-radius-one cases: pathwise uniqueness for the stochastic problem and remaining two-dimensional deterministic cases.。
- 适用范围。The stated results are for the orthant with constant face reflection and the paper's spectral/dimensional hypotheses; not a blanket reflected-diffusion uniqueness rule.。

## Marked Point Processes in Discrete Time

- 原件 49a57a5f92879f5f99fda8ceb2c5929006eca1cfce81d6942bec7cb36e0f611e。Queueing Systems (2019), DOI 10.1007/s11134-019-09612-3。物理页 1, 2。
- discrete-probability。Abstract and §1, physical PDF pp. 1–2。
- 已有技能。The published article develops equivalent time, interarrival and sequence representations for stationary discrete-time marked point processes, then discrete Palm/inversion/Campbell formulas and a periodic-stationary Little-law application.。
- 适用范围。The framework permits batch arrivals and uses stationarity; it is not a continuous-time PASTA theorem and each Palm identity needs its own integrability and stationarity hypotheses.。
- 相关技能。[probability-renewal-palm-coupling](../../../../probability-renewal-palm-coupling/SKILL.md)。

## Stationary coupling method for renewal process in continuous time: application to strong bounds for the convergence rate of the distribution of the regenerative process

- 原件 aadcdd118ec41613c65589bec9b59c35927e1015bca6efed16257e8bb18c4ee9。arXiv:1704.04808v2 [math.PR], 21 December 2017; manuscript dated 21 June 2018。物理页 1, 18。
- Renewal/regenerative-process stationary coupling。Abstract and stated application on p. 1; Theorem 1 and moment bound for the coupling epoch on p. 18.。
- 已有技能。It constructs a stationary coupling for a backward renewal process and, under its Key Condition plus finite K-th moments, obtains finite coupling-time moments used for total-variation convergence bounds of regenerative processes.。
- 适用范围。The result is tied to the constructed backward-renewal coupling and its Key Condition; no universal polynomial rate follows merely from regenerative structure.。
- 相关技能。[probability-renewal-palm-coupling](../../../../probability-renewal-palm-coupling/SKILL.md)。

## Bounding the Number of Self-Avoiding Walks: Hammersley-Welsh with Polygon Insertion

- 原件 a9fa6fda92abda1a20eca0f378b1565e37e8afa69f58a2a57acc0482b270d8f3。author manuscript; first-page metadata inspected。物理页 1, 2。
- probability-other。Abstract and introduction (physical pp. 1–2)。
- 已有技能。Treats the connective-constant upper-bound problem for self-avoiding walks and describes a polygon-insertion route to improving the classical Hammersley–Welsh exponential correction in the planar setting.。
- 适用范围。The exact lattice assumptions and exponent improvement are theorem-level details not certified by these first two pages alone.。

## Bounds on connective constants of regular graphs

- 原件 6c51e9b417bf6ac4817731e6d2d9695ea577a72b5fa7daefcca03406c2bd1bed。Manuscript dated 23 October 2012, minor revision 6 April 2013; no formal publication imprint visible on consulted page。物理页 1。
- Self-avoiding-walk connective constants。Abstract, definition, and Theorem 1.1 on p. 1.。
- 已有技能。For an infinite connected simple vertex-transitive Delta-regular graph, the stated theorem gives sqrt(Delta−1)≤mu(G), alongside the standard upper bound mu(G)≤Delta−1.。
- 适用范围。Vertex transitivity, simplicity, regularity, and infinitude are part of the theorem. No planar-SLE conclusion follows from this graph-growth bound.。
- 相关技能。[probability-self-avoiding-walks-and-sle](../../../../probability-self-avoiding-walks-and-sle/SKILL.md)。

## Connective Constants of Grigorchuk Graphs

- 原件 6503fb5b5d89e4d21e4b7ab9ce1bfcad65234b0554cd419c53098e7da021e451。arXiv:2608.19349v2 (2026-10-07; source dated 2026-10-01 revision)。物理页 1, 2。
- Self-avoiding walks on Grigorchuk-group Cayley graphs。Abstract, Theorem 1.2, and introduction, physical pp. 1–2.。
- 已有技能。For three-generator Cayley graphs of general Grigorchuk groups, the paper proves connective constant at least the golden mean, strictly above it when the coding sequence contains all three symbols.。
- 适用范围。The theorem is for this family of Cayley graphs and its stated coding condition; it does not resolve the broader conjecture for all infinite transitive cubic graphs.。
- 相关技能。[probability-self-avoiding-walks-and-sle](../../../../probability-self-avoiding-walks-and-sle/SKILL.md)。

## Convergence of the Self-Avoiding Walk on Random Quadrangulations to SLE 8/3 on sqrt(8/3)-Liouville Quantum Gravity

- 原件 861ea5f8700e5b213b17bfc5a0b8f961306d1abdd1ef3514dde32d3a379ccfda。preprint; version/date not visible on inspected opening pages。物理页 1, 2, 3。
- self-avoiding walk and random planar maps。p.1 abstract; pp.2–3 introduction; contents locate main proof。
- 已有技能。The paper proves scaling convergence for specified UIHPQ/whole-plane quadrangulations decorated by a self-avoiding walk, identifying the limit via metric gluing of Brownian half-planes and, with cited work, SLE 8/3 on sqrt(8/3)-LQG.。
- 适用范围。The proof concerns the explicitly defined random quadrangulations and local Gromov–Hausdorff–Prokhorov–uniform topology; it is not a scaling-limit proof for lattice SAW.。
- 相关技能。[probability-self-avoiding-walks-and-sle](../../../../probability-self-avoiding-walks-and-sle/SKILL.md)。

## Critical two-point functions and the lace expansion for spread-out high-dimensional percolation and related models

- 原件 f7b394e4a5ba6a746fbc3ee511c8627f68fb9f75aecbdff911da500b729bc35d。arXiv:math-ph/0011046v1 (28 Nov 2000)。物理页 1, 2, 4。
- spread-out high-dimensional lace expansion。PDF p. 1 abstract; p. 2 contents; p. 4 begins the main-result setup for the spread-out kernel.。
- 已有技能。For sufficiently spread-out finite-range models above their respective upper critical dimensions, the paper uses an x-space lace-expansion scheme to obtain critical two-point decay proportional to |x|^(2−d) for SAW, percolation, lattice trees, and animals.。
- 适用范围。The statement depends on the specified spread-out kernel and large-spread parameter, and differs from nearest-neighbour and low-dimensional models. It is not an SLE or generic correlation-decay theorem.。
- 相关技能。[probability-self-avoiding-walks-and-sle](../../../../probability-self-avoiding-walks-and-sle/SKILL.md)。

## Decay of correlations in nearest-neighbor self-avoiding walk, percolation, lattice trees and animals

- 原件 34368f98803701fe89ea64bc7f7d39c7f527815b30d65c11062b8e8c04f19008。Annals of Probability 36 (2008), 530–593, DOI 10.1214/009117907000000231; arXiv:math-ph/0504021v3。物理页 1, 3。
- nearest-neighbour high-dimensional lace expansion。PDF p. 1 abstract and stated dimension ranges; p. 3 model definitions and Theorem 1.1 opening.。
- 已有技能。Using lace expansion, this paper obtains critical two-point asymptotics proportional to |x|^(2−d) for nearest-neighbour SAW in d≥5, percolation in d≥19, and trees/animals only in sufficiently high dimension.。
- 适用范围。These are model- and dimension-dependent nearest-neighbour results. The percolation d≥19 threshold and the separate large-d clause must not be weakened or confused with spread-out results.。
- 相关技能。[probability-self-avoiding-walks-and-sle](../../../../probability-self-avoiding-walks-and-sle/SKILL.md)。

## Existence of a Non-Averaging Regime for the Self-Avoiding Walk on a High-Dimensional Infinite Percolation Cluster

- 原件 313cb1b9c440371cf59559d6d07ef9260170c184fa6bb3d2cb88556c40301457。arXiv:1212.4641v2 (26 Jun 2013)。物理页 1, 2。
- discrete-probability。Abstract and introduction, physical PDF pp. 1–2。
- 已有技能。For a supercritical Bernoulli cluster in sufficiently high dimension, the manuscript states a p-range (p_c,p_c^(2)) where the quenched limsup connective rate is strictly below the annealed exponential rate.。
- 适用范围。It is a high-dimensional p-range statement about a limsup and does not give a planar result, a full nth-root limit, or an arbitrary-disorder transfer.。

- self-avoiding walks on directed quasi-transitive graphs。Abstract and §1 introduction (PDF pp. 1–2)。

## Non-coincidence of Quenched and Annealed Connective Constants on the Supercritical Planar Percolation Cluster

- 原件 1a681dbf7831f0f5ecf8d555083fbf60367592e550d11cd4a384566182bbcb69。arXiv:1203.6051v3 (21 Jul 2013)。物理页 1, 2。
- discrete-probability。Abstract and §1, physical PDF pp. 1–2。
- 已有技能。For p in (1/2,1) on the planar supercritical cluster, the manuscript states an almost-sure strict gap between the quenched limsup exponential count of self-avoiding paths and its annealed counterpart, using change of measure and coarse graining.。

## A Sufficient Condition for the Existence of an Invariant Probability Measure for Markov Processes

- 原件 1ae7e95f656a1ceecbb4a522075752145e5b6dfb4ce5bdbaf3234af06039f2f1。Journal of Applied Probability 42 (2005), pp. 873–878。物理页 1, 2。
- Foster–Lyapunov invariant-measure existence。Abstract and §1 introduction (PDF pp. 1–2)。
- 已有技能。Shows that a Foster–Lyapunov criterion suffices for existence of an invariant probability measure for discrete- and continuous-time Markov processes without added irreducibility assumptions.。
- 适用范围。It is an existence criterion under the paper's drift/test-set conditions; it does not imply uniqueness, ergodicity, or convergence rates.。
- 相关技能。[probability-stationary-law-interfaces](../../../../probability-stationary-law-interfaces/SKILL.md)。

## Einstein relation for biased random walk on Galton--Watson trees

- 原件 09d23a27cd627d1ab86119ee664a3e1fdea92fddbbcf2f68bf05b48494f8896a。arXiv:1106.4387v2 [math.PR], 22 December 2011。物理页 1, 2。
- Velocity response of biased random walks on random trees。Galton--Watson and exponential-moment assumptions on p. 1; Theorem 1.1 on p. 2.。
- 已有技能。For the paper's no-leaf Galton--Watson environment with mean m>1 and an exponential offspring moment, it proves lim_{alpha downarrow 0} vbar_alpha/alpha=D0/2 for the specified continuous-time bias perturbation.。
- 适用范围。The normalization of bias, continuous-time clock, and tree-law assumptions determine the factor. It is not a generic fluctuation-dissipation theorem for stationary random environments.。
- 相关技能。[probability-stationary-law-interfaces](../../../../probability-stationary-law-interfaces/SKILL.md)。

- Piecewise-deterministic Markov processes and invariant measures。Abstract and model introduction, physical pp. 1–2.。
- 已有技能。The paper studies switched-semiflow PDMPs with jumps, proving an exponentially attracting invariant measure and strong laws for the post-jump chain and transferring invariant measures to the continuous-time process.。

## On the Existence and Uniqueness of Stationary Distributions for Some Piecewise Deterministic Markov Processes with State-Dependent Jump Intensity

- 原件 107dcf81d0822f72ae81b09409a57de0709d61f9cc01f2e4673e1247beba6fe9。arXiv:2303.11576v5 (2024-05-26)。物理页 1, 2, 3, 4。

- 已有技能。For the defined state-dependent-intensity PDMP subclass, it establishes a correspondence between stationary distributions of the continuous process and of its post-jump Markov chain, then derives existence/uniqueness conditions.。
